# Legal clearance remains open before release

Hold release clearance until counsel reviews the commercial data restrictions, photo derivatives, real institution depictions, and AI provenance. The fictional-officeholder rule is implemented as an intention, but real governors’ names remain in calibration data and generated names can match living officeholders. Federal statistics generally have a usable basis; that basis does not cover every research publisher or reference image. This is legal research and a draft risk review, not legal advice or an attorney’s approval. No game code or assets changed. An attorney must review the release build and its marketing before release.

## Fictional officeholders are intended, but absence is not confirmed

**Measured:** The owner’s fictional-officeholder ruling appears in [Progress](../roadmap/PROGRESS.md). The opening code explicitly describes people and initial tenures as fictional in [opening-officeholders](../../src/presentation/opening-officeholders.ts#L37). It calls the generated-name machinery instead of establishing real politicians as those people.

**Measured:** The calibration JSON contains Gavin Newsom and Ron DeSantis, among other real governors, in [electoral calibration](../../src/simulation/world-setup/electoral-calibration.generated.json#L8343). The production initializer imports that JSON in [political-start](../../src/simulation/world-setup/political-start.ts#L5). A TypeScript type assertion does not remove unused fields from the imported object.

No production read of `officeholderName` was located in the examined TypeScript. This supports fictional character creation; it does not prove that real names are absent from a shipped bundle. The same JSON contains election footnotes naming Jon Ossoff and Raphael G. Warnock. A release engineer should remove identifying fields and notes from the shipped projection while preserving research originals and their hashes.

**Measured:** The [NGA captures](../../data/source/electoral-calibration-2024/raw/nga/governors-index.html) contain real governor names and portrait links. The [movement evidence](../../data/research/normal-year-movement/movement-evidence.json#L165) explicitly names Governor Gavin Newsom. FJC judiciary captures contain real judges’ identities and careers. These are research references, not evidence that those people appear as game characters.

**Measured:** Given names and surnames are drawn independently in [people](../../src/simulation/people.ts#L693). The gendered path also selects independently and has no current-officeholder exclusion in [the generated-name path](../../src/simulation/people.ts#L780). Census and SSA name origins do not prevent a real full-name match.

**Measured:** The [starter lists](../../src/simulation/names-data.ts) contain 346 given-name strings and 697 surname strings. A pool-intersection audit found 66 two-part comparison-roster strings whose first and last parts both occur in those lists. Examples include Elizabeth Warren, Mark Kelly, John Kennedy, Alex Padilla, Brian Kemp, and Sarah McBride. The method below defines that intersection; it does not measure observed characters or collision probabilities.

**Measured search:** The comparison covered 5,098 tracked text files across source code, data, art metadata, and public assets, totaling 852,696,971 bytes. After boundary filtering, 986 distinct file-line-name records remained across 84 files. Those are text matches, not 986 politicians or 84 confirmed depictions. Homonyms, historical names, institutional names, and biographies require classification.

No boundary-valid comparison match remained in art metadata. Production matches outside calibration included the historical school-name example Daniel Webster.

**Measured:** The [public education catalog](../../public/education/catalog-b86e925cc2cc2c22a07903613b50b5569e7737ffc8114009a9d510f78fad6daf.json) includes Dr. Frederica S. Wilson/Skyway Elementary School, Henry Cuellar Elementary, and John Hoeven Elementary School. These name institutions, not fictional officeholders. They nevertheless prevent a blanket claim that no living politician’s name appears in shipped assets.

The comparison roster combines date-filtered congressional terms from [Congress Legislators](https://github.com/unitedstates/congress-legislators/blob/main/legislators-current.yaml), governors in the project’s calibration, and selected federal executives. The roster is a secondary index. Its dates and governor entries need fresh official confirmation. It does not cover every current state legislator, mayor, territorial officer, judge, nickname, spelling variant, or recently changed office.

**Measured visual scope:** Contact sheets covered 345 files selected by head, face, or human-candidate filenames, all 430 manifest backdrops, and all 52 manifest campuses. A closer view covered 36 base smiling faces. No obvious intentional depiction of a current officeholder was recognized in that review. Small wall portraits, logos, other image families, and final composite scenes were not cleared at release resolution.

**Inferred risk:** A matching name alone does not establish appropriation or defamation. Combining a real name, recognizable face, office, and fictional wrongdoing increases identification and endorsement concerns. Public figures do not waive all publicity, privacy, or defamation protections. Expressive-game defenses and state publicity rules need counsel’s analysis; fictional intent and a disclaimer are insufficient by themselves.

For the owner’s stricter creative rule, add a maintained exclusion check to future name generation and audit ordinary generated worlds before release. Review portraits and prompts against current official rosters. Keep historical research citations separate from character identity and marketing. This report cannot confirm “no real living politicians appear.”

## Data permissions depend on the actual work and publisher

**Measured inventory:** The tracked source and research directories contain 884 files. The coverage tables below include every top-level directory and standalone research item, including sources outside the central manifest. The citation register records individual URLs found in metadata and corpora. A URL, an acquisition hash, and an owner approval are not a license.

[17 U.S.C. § 105](https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/17/105) excludes qualifying U.S. government works from domestic copyright protection, subject to statutory exceptions. It does not make every government-hosted work public domain. Contractor works, third-party photos, and works transferred to the government can retain copyright. It does not automatically apply to state governments, foreign distribution, trademarks, or access contracts.

[17 U.S.C. § 102(b)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/17/102) distinguishes unprotected ideas and systems from protected expression. Factual values can be used differently from reproducing tables, questionnaires, explanatory prose, or original compilations. Attribution does not replace permission. A transformed simulation parameter is not automatically exempt from an acquisition contract.

The [government-edicts decision](https://www.supremecourt.gov/opinions/19pdf/18-1150_7m58.pdf) supports reuse of lawmaking and judicial work within its doctrine. Do not extend it to every state website, agency handbook, vendor annotation, or municipal meeting packet. Use official statutory facts and independently written explanations where possible. Preserve section numbers, jurisdiction, effective date, and source citations for accuracy.

### Federal statistics and required or requested credits

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Publisher and project products | License basis and exceptions | Attribution duty and release action |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Census: ACS tables and PUMS, county citizenship, housing and commute, household mix, voting registration, PEP places and counties, CBSA, TIGER boundaries and relationships, CBP, Economic Census, government finances, employment, school finances, SIPP | Qualifying federal statistical products generally fall under § 105. Verify product notices and any incorporated material. Public-use files are distinct from restricted files. | [Census citation guidance](https://www.census.gov/about/policies/citation.html) requests agency, product, table or file, vintage, URL, and access date. Credit the original observations; label the game’s calculations and historical estimates as its own. |
| BLS: LAUS, OEWS, CPS, pay periods, Consumer Expenditure, employment context | [BLS copyright policy](https://www.bls.gov/opub/copyright-information.htm) treats its publications as public domain except identified previously copyrighted photos or illustrations. | BLS requests source citation. Preserve series, geography, period, and revision. Its emblem is a protected registered mark; statistical permission does not authorize it. |
| BEA regional accounts and income; EIA energy; NOAA storm events; FEMA disasters; HUD fair-market rents; FTA transit | Federal-authorship basis, subject to dataset notices. [BEA’s policy](https://www.bea.gov/help/faq/147) identifies website information as public domain unless stated otherwise. | Retain publisher, dataset, release vintage, retrieval date, and modifications. Confirm product-specific exceptions and foreign rights before global distribution. |
| NCES CCD, IPEDS, EDGE, tuition, school and college directories | Public statistical and institution-directory files have a federal basis. The [Department of Education notice](https://www.ed.gov/about/ed-overview/required-notices/website-policies/copyright-status-notice) excludes identified third-party content. | Credit NCES, program, file, and year. Institution names and coordinates are not student records. Do not imply that a current directory verifies historical school names or simulated events. |
| CMS Hospital General Information and Provider of Services | Public facility-level files support a federal factual basis. [CMS website policy](https://www.cms.gov/about-cms/web-policies-important-links/about-website/link-to-us) and [disclaimers](https://www.cms.gov/disclaimers) do not license every incorporated code system. | Credit CMS, exact files, release quarter, and transformations. The examined inputs concern providers, not patient records. Exclude copyrighted CPT or other incorporated code sets unless separately licensed. |
| O*NET occupation, task, and crosswalk inputs | The locks declare CC BY 4.0. [O*NET’s current database license](https://www.onetcenter.org/license_db.html) supports that basis for covered database content. Check the particular crosswalk download’s scope. | Required: acknowledge O*NET version and the U.S. Department of Labor/Employment and Training Administration, link CC BY 4.0, preserve applicable notices, and identify modifications. Observe separate O*NET trademark rules. |
| SHEEO State Higher Education Finance | [SHEF’s download page](https://shef.sheeo.org/data-downloads/) expressly permits use with appropriate attribution and citation. This is not a federal-work assumption. | Cite State Higher Education Executive Officers Association, State Higher Education Finance, fiscal year and publication year. Verify any separately licensed adjustment inputs before redistributing them. |

Some Census locks mark attribution as required; others mark it false. That inconsistency should be reconciled with actual notices and the requested citation practice. The safest credits policy is to preserve citations across all federal-derived products without representing the agency as endorsing the game.

### Commercial sources, polls, and papers remain partly unresolved

**Verified restriction:** [Book of the States terms](https://bookofthestates.org/terms-of-use/) limit licensed data use to noncommercial purposes and restrict substantial copying. They require conspicuous Council of State Governments source acknowledgment. The stored legislative, governor, and judge salary tables therefore lack a demonstrated paid-game license. Obtain written commercial rights or independently source the underlying salary facts from official records. Counsel should assess existing capture and redistribution rights as well as compiled outputs.

**Measured:** The movement research cites Gallup’s Biden approval series and a Berkeley IGS Newsom poll. [Gallup’s permissions page](https://www.gallup.com/corporate/198173/gallup-permissions.aspx) requires permission for covered republication. Do not ship its charts, branded tables, question text, or report prose based on a citation alone. Counsel should distinguish using facts for internal calibration from redistributing protected material and review applicable access terms.

The [Berkeley IGS item](https://escholarship.org/uc/item/26b6t6k0) was blocked by a browser challenge. Its item-specific license was not verified. Public access through eScholarship does not establish CC licensing. Other poll references, including Pew or YouGov where present, remain item-specific and unresolved unless an express license or written permission is saved.

**Measured:** Research citations include NBER, AEA, Oxford, SAGE, Chicago, INFORMS, Springer, and PubMed Central papers. The item register below includes the detected paper links. Their individual text, figure, questionnaire, and replication-data licenses were not established. A paper’s presence on a federal host does not make its authors’ work a federal government work.

Use independently written factual research summaries with authors, title, publication, year, DOI or URL, and a precise locator. Obtain rights before distributing paper text, tables, illustrations, or supplements. Check replication-data and code licenses separately, including noncommercial clauses. Citation-only research and shipped content require different clearance decisions; the build inventory must establish which applies.

NGA is an association, not a federal agency. Its HTML captures include copyrighted presentation, biographies, and portrait links alongside factual office information. NCSL, Ballotpedia, LegiScan, CSG, Tax Foundation, KFF, NAR, Zillow, MIT Living Wage, news outlets, and other cited organizations likewise need their own terms review. State website access does not confer a blanket redistribution license.

Wikipedia text and Wikimedia images have different licenses. If Wikipedia prose is reproduced or adapted, verify the applicable text license and contributor attribution. If it is merely a locator for official facts, cite the primary source. Municipal code vendors’ interface, editorial materials, and access terms remain separate from the laws they display.

The owner-provided judicial-selection source expressly states task authorization without broader redistribution terms in [its lock](../../data/source/judicial-office-selection/artifact-lock.json). Internal Drive research also lacks public distribution evidence. Obtain the author’s rights grant and third-party source lineage before shipping their expression. Authored estimates require contributor ownership records and source citations where derived.

### Coverage of every source-directory unit

The status below is research classification, not clearance.

**F** means a conditional federal-work basis with product exceptions and requested citation.

**E** means government-edicts analysis for the law itself, with source citation and separate host terms.

**C** means a specific licensed or permitted source.

**R** means a verified restriction.

**U** means permission and attribution terms remain unresolved. Mixed rows require artifact-level separation.

The authors should provide title, owner, terms, attribution, intended use, and shipped derivative for each item they intend to distribute. The citation register preserves the individual links rather than treating research directories as licenses.

## Generated artwork does not clear its reference inputs

**Measured:** The [art provenance registry](../../art/manifest/provenance.json) has 134 entries. Eighty-eight record unknown rights; 46 record owned rights. Several generator names and executed settings are unrecorded. An approval field establishes creative approval, not legal ownership. One claimed Firefly return records an embedded Google AI credit, so filenames cannot establish the executed model.

### Firefly terms and evidence to retain

[Adobe’s Firefly FAQ](https://helpx.adobe.com/firefly/web/get-started/learn-the-basics/adobe-firefly-faq.html) permits commercial use of non-beta outputs. Beta output is generally permitted unless the product states otherwise. Record the actual product, model, generation date, account, plan, applicable terms, prompts, reference inputs, export, and human edits. The asset registry also lists Gemini, Scenario, and OpenAI; those assets need their own applicable terms.

The [Generative AI Product Specific Terms](https://www.adobe.com/go/adobe-gen-ai-addl-terms), effective April 23, 2026, place input and output responsibility on the user. They prohibit removing or altering generated watermarks or Content Credentials. They do not warrant noninfringement, uniqueness, or protectability. Non-Adobe models invoke separate third-party rules.

The same terms restrict indemnity to specified eligible teams or enterprise plans and qualifying features, surfaces, and export events. Exclusions include modifications, combinations, certain reference inputs, and context. Do not assume that cropped, composited game assets carry indemnity. Counsel must review the actual agreement and workflow; the public terms alone do not establish coverage.

The [AI user guidelines](https://www.adobe.com/legal/licenses-terms/adobe-gen-ai-user-guidelines.html) prohibit infringing inputs and deceptive impersonation. Preserve original metadata and source-to-derivative lineage. Audit conversion and packaging for removed credentials. No general obligation to print an Adobe credit was identified in these materials; contractual metadata duties and source-photo credits still apply.

The [Copyright Office’s AI report](https://www.copyright.gov/ai/Copyright-and-Artificial-Intelligence-Part-2-Copyrightability-Report.pdf) distinguishes human authorship from AI-generated expression. Commercial permission does not establish exclusive copyright in every output. Document human contributions and contributor assignments where protection or ownership matters.

### Capitol, landmark, and university reference photos

**Measured:** The backdrop manifest records 123 photo-linked derivatives from 56 distinct photos. The campus manifest records 52 derivatives from 52 distinct photos. All 108 distinct recorded photo URLs are Wikimedia Commons file pages. No non-Wikimedia photo URL appears in those two photo fields. That finding does not cover unrecorded inputs or other art families.

The remaining 307 backdrop entries do not record a photo license in that manifest. That is a provenance gap, not proof that each uses an unlicensed photo. Some may be generated without photo inputs. Reconcile the generators, prompts, original sources, and deployed files before clearance.

The per-photo table below reproduces the recorded author and license for every linked photo and identifies every derivative filename. Most file-page grants were not independently verified. The Berkeley campus page was checked and lists 0BSD; its original publisher grant should also be retained. A manifest license string alone is not a completed clearance record.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Recorded license | Commercial-use and attribution implications | Derivative concern |
| --- | --- | --- |
| CC BY 2.0, 2.5, 3.0, or 4.0 | Commercial use is allowed subject to the particular license. Provide creator, source, license version and link, applicable title and notices, and changes. | Mark cropping, color changes, AI transformation, and resizing. Review the exact version and any jurisdiction-specific port. |
| CC BY-SA 2.0, 3.0, or 4.0 | Attribution applies. Share adapted material under the permitted license for that version. | Decide whether the generated picture adapts protected photo expression. ShareAlike does not automatically license the entire game’s unrelated code. Counsel should review asset licensing, EULA exclusions, and Steam packaging. |
| Public domain or CC0 | No copyright attribution duty under a valid waiver or applicable public-domain basis; voluntary provenance credit remains useful. | Verify the basis, jurisdictions, and author. Neither classification clears trademarks, publicity, or separate works depicted. |
| 0BSD | The recorded Berkeley grant permits commercial modification and distribution without an attribution condition. | Retain the grant and original-source evidence. Check that the photo is actually covered by the publisher’s grant. |

The [Commons reuse guide](https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/Commons:Reusing_content_outside_Wikimedia) requires attention to each file’s license and other restrictions. Commons hosting does not guarantee clearance. The [CC BY-SA 4.0 legal code](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/legalcode.en) also prohibits imposing effective technological measures or extra terms that impair licensed rights. Counsel should decide how that interacts with depot protection and the game EULA.

Supply a distributed credits file and accessible credits entry for cleared third-party assets. Include original title, creator, original file-page URL, exact license link, notices, and a plain description of changes. Retain source copies, hashes, license snapshots, and parent-to-child mappings. AI regeneration is not a dependable way to eliminate a reference photo’s rights.

[17 U.S.C. § 120(a)](https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/17/120) provides a U.S. exception for pictorial representations of qualifying architecture visible from public places. It does not authorize copying a photographer’s protected composition or separately protected sculptures, murals, or interiors. Territorial and foreign rules require separate review. Interior plans and official photos in [environment evidence](../../src/environment/reference/evidence.json) need independent permission checks.

## Real institutions and places need accurate, limited depictions

**Measured:** The [institution types](../../src/simulation/local-institutions-data.ts#L1) expressly model sourced real names. Their data include NCES schools and colleges, CMS hospitals, and Census places. The CMS input names Mayo Clinic Hospital and Cleveland Clinic Hospital. The campus manifest names universities including Harvard, Cornell, and the University of Alaska Fairbanks.

Institution and place names are factual identifiers; they can also function as trademarks. Descriptive use in an expressive game can differ from using a mark as a badge, endorsement, or selling point. [15 U.S.C. § 1125](https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/15/1125) addresses confusion over affiliation or sponsorship and related claims. A federal directory does not grant the institution’s logo rights.

**Inferred risk:** Assigning fictional malpractice, corruption, discriminatory conduct, or crime to an identifiable real school, hospital, employer, or college can create defamation or disparagement concerns. It can also misleadingly describe real services. Label the simulation clearly and have counsel review harmful institution-specific events and marketing examples.

**Measured:** The [school-row comment](../../src/simulation/local-institutions-data.ts#L8) identifies current names carried backward before the directory vintage as estimates. **Inferred:** Historical accuracy needs source verification wherever those names are presented as real past attendance.

**Measured:** The [campus manifest](../../art/campuses/manifest.json#L16) records named flagship artwork as standing in for other institution kinds. **Inferred:** A recognizable Harvard or Berkeley landmark used for an unrelated private college can create affiliation confusion. Prefer accurate institution binding or generic, independently created scenery. Obtain permission for prominent branding where counsel determines it is needed.

**Measured:** The [civic-symbol manifest](../../art/manifest/civic_symbols.json) says symbol artwork has not been acquired. Some backdrop [surface metadata](../../art/backdrops/surfaces.json#L484) excludes seals from specified surfaces. The thumbnails nevertheless show flags, round wall emblems, and recognizable government interiors. Metadata exclusion does not prove that generated pixels contain no protected seal.

[18 U.S.C. § 713](https://www.law.cornell.edu/uscode/text/18/713) regulates uses of specified federal seals and their likenesses, including endorsement implications. State seals have separate statutes. Flags, institutional crests, mascots, uniforms, vehicle markings, campaign logos, commercial signage, and store capsules need a rendered-image check. A photo’s copyright license does not settle those uses.

No obvious commercial wordmark or current-officeholder portrait was identified in the contact sheets. This limited finding is not a logo clearance. Complete an asset-resolution and in-game review, especially oval-office, governor-office, university, rally, television, hospital, and storefront scenes. Counsel should also check the game title and marketing marks before commercial use; no trademark registry search was completed here.

## Steam needs rights evidence and an accurate AI disclosure

The [Steam Content Survey](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/gettingstarted/contentsurvey) is required before store-page and build review. Its AI questions cover player-consumed art, audio, narrative, and localization, with a distinction for development-efficiency tools. Disclose the project’s pre-generated artwork, including edited AI-assisted outputs.

The publisher must represent that pre-generated content is lawful, noninfringing, and consistent with marketing. Live-generated AI adds guardrail obligations. The offline name and English machinery does not establish a runtime model service. Inspect the actual build before answering that question. Some later survey changes require Steam Support.

A draft disclosure is: “We use pre-generated AI-assisted artwork for character components and environments.” Expand it to other verified player content. Describe human review and runtime behavior only after verifying them.

[Steam onboarding rules](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/gettingstarted/onboarding) prohibit inadequate-rights content, defamation, unlawful content in offered jurisdictions, and other specified material. The publishing entity must have rights to publish and complete legal identification, bank, tax, fee, and agreement requirements. The current page lists a 21-day initial waiting period and at least two weeks of a public Coming Soon page. Confirm current account-specific timing before scheduling release.

Store pages and builds require separate review under [Steam’s review process](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/store/review_process). Match screenshots, capsules, trailers, supported platforms, and feature claims to the actual reviewed build. Complete content ratings and age-gating accurately. If sold in Early Access, follow the [Early Access rules](https://partner.steamgames.com/doc/store/earlyaccess), including accurate present functionality and required disclosures.

An attorney must review the executed Steam Distribution Agreement, publisher rights chain, EULA, required license exceptions, and distribution territories. The private agreement was not available here. Public Steam documentation and a draft survey sentence do not establish contractual compliance. This report does not submit a survey or publish a store page.

## What must happen before release clearance

The data owner should separate reusable federal facts from licensed third-party expression and restricted acquisitions. Resolve CSG commercial rights first. Add exact O*NET and SHEF credits. Supply per-item poll and paper terms, contributor grants, and a list distinguishing research-only evidence from shipped content.

The art owner should reconcile every deployed file to its generator and reference inputs. Verify the 108 photo grants, resolve unknown provenance, retain required credentials, and prepare credits. Counsel should review ShareAlike adaptations, institutional depictions, seals, and recognizable people before replacement or licensing decisions.

The release owner should compare the final depot, generated characters, and marketing against refreshed official rosters and this rights register. Remove real officeholder identity fields from the shipped projection in a separately authorized implementation task. Complete the Steam survey and deliver the executed agreements and actual build to the attorney. Attorney review precedes release even where a row below says routine remediation can be done without counsel.

## Method, validation, and cost

The review follows [AGENTS.md](../../AGENTS.md) and the [civic-reports skill](../../.agents/skills/civic-reports/SKILL.md). The user’s instruction supplies the legal-review scope. The existing registered workspace was reused. No package build or asset modification was performed. Pinned validation tools were fetched into a temporary cache; project dependencies were unchanged.

The local snapshot is `f20299099d`; the draft branch was created from remote main `360217261436f78eae6d201b3753b6d9be279e08`. GitHub comparison showed only nine documentation-file differences between those heads. The examined game code, data, and art therefore matched that remote base. Terms and public guidance were checked on October 11, 2026; generation-time terms may differ.

Citation extraction covered tracked metadata and textual corpora in both requested data roots. Acquisition locks and manifests identify binary archives and raw captures; embedded navigation links in raw HTML were not treated as new data licenses. The register includes observed links, not a warranty that each is reachable or correctly cited. Unlinked files and owner research remain covered by their unit’s unresolved status.

The officeholder comparison used roster blob `54325e0e373bcaa647037d731e46405d6e371f89` and terms spanning October 11, 2026. The 789 comparison strings were searched in their recorded, uppercase, and lowercase forms, then filtered at alphabetic boundaries. Mixed capitalization, likenesses, aliases, and unlisted local officers remain outside that check. Visual contact sheets were temporary review artifacts and were not added to the game.

The pool audit extracted the starter arrays’ quoted strings and counted distinct values in each array. It selected roster strings containing exactly two space-separated parts. A string counted as a possible collision when its first part belonged to the given-name set and its second part belonged to the surname set. Gender-specific pools and generated-world frequencies were not measured.

The Census copyright URL failed to load; the federal statute and accessible citation guidance support the narrower federal-work analysis. The Berkeley poll and one PMC page were browser-challenged. The attempted NBER copyright page was inaccessible. Their item rights remain unresolved. No blocked page is described as permission.

Validation: `npm run report:check -- docs/reports/2026-10-legal-review.md` passed with zero errors and warnings before independent review.

`npm run zero-dice` passed, with 193 existing allowed lines. The standard PR release command initially could not start because the project’s `tsx` installation was absent. The first storage-wrapper attempt also used an unsupported operation name. The same release-check entry point passed when launched with pinned temporary `tsx` through the supported storage reservation.

No TypeScript, JavaScript, or player-facing screen changed, so code typechecking, ESLint, and changed-code tests do not apply to this change. Pinned Prettier formatted the three changed Markdown files. The release-entry command was `npm exec --package=tsx@4.23.12 -- tsx scripts/release/index.ts check --mode pr`, with the temporary cache and storage reservation. Gameplay, the game bench, and a release build were not tested.

The GitHub Project claim could not be recorded through the available connector tools. A CLI fallback also failed to resolve the owner type. The draft branch and PR record LEGAL-CHECK ownership and the review contract. No merge or release is authorized by this review.

Cost record: Codex, GPT-6 family; the exact deployment model ID and provider effort setting were not exposed. The repository default is medium, appropriate for bounded research and documentation. No model escalation occurred. Token or billing usage was not visible.

## Risk decisions for the release attorney

Severity describes release exposure, not a conclusion that liability exists. Critical means a demonstrated permission conflict or an unsupported release representation. High means substantial rights or identity uncertainty. Medium means a bounded issue requiring evidence or correction. Low means routine documentation with a plausible reuse basis.

<!-- prettier-ignore -->
| Risk | Severity | What to fix | Needs an attorney? |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| CSG salary-table commercial restriction | Critical | Obtain written commercial rights or replace acquisition and derivatives with independently sourced official facts. Review stored captures. | Yes: license, contract, and existing distribution. |
| Real governor names in runtime-imported calibration | High | Create a shipped projection without identity or portrait fields. Preserve research originals. Inspect the final bundle. | Yes for release review; engineering can implement the separation. |
| Generated names can match living officeholders | High | Maintain current exclusions, check aliases and local offices, and audit generated characters with their portraits and offices. | Yes for identity and publicity standards. |
| Officeholder likenesses not exhaustively cleared | High | Review all final portraits, prompts, composites, wall pictures, and marketing at release resolution. | Yes if recognizable people remain. |
| Polls, papers, NGA captures, and other commercial research have unresolved terms | High | Record each item’s grant and intended use. Obtain permission for protected reproductions or replace them with independent factual summaries. | Yes for uncertain contract, fair-use, and compilation questions. |
| Owner-provided and contributor research lacks redistribution grants | High | Obtain author grants, source lineage, and assignments where needed. | Yes where rights ownership or incorporated works are unclear. |
| CC photo adaptation and ShareAlike duties | High | Verify file grants, credit each source, mark changes, and resolve adapted-asset licensing, EULA, and packaging. | Yes for derivative and distribution analysis. |
| Unknown AI provenance or lost Content Credentials | High | Establish actual model, account, plan, generation terms, input rights, originals, metadata, and derivative lineage. | Yes for contractual exceptions or uncertain grants. |
| Real institution names paired with harmful fictional conduct or unrelated campus art | High | Correct bindings, use generic scenery where appropriate, and review identifying events and marketing. | Yes for defamation, disparagement, and affiliation concerns. |
| Seals, logos, mascots, signage, and marketing marks | High | Audit rendered pixels and title marks. Obtain necessary permissions or replace restricted designs. | Yes for statutory and trademark scope. |
| Unsupported Steam rights or AI survey answers | Critical | Complete accurate pre-generated and any live-generated disclosure against the actual depot. Supply rights evidence and guardrails if applicable. | Yes for agreements and release representations; survey entry is operational. |
| O*NET, SHEF, and other mandatory credits | Medium | Add exact source/version/license credits, modification notices, and applicable trademark notices. | Usually no for routine credits; yes for disputed scope. |
| Federal-product exceptions and inaccurate historical institution facts | Medium | Verify product notices, omit third-party code sets, retain citations, and identify the game’s estimates. | Yes for exceptions or foreign-rights questions. |
| Steam store claims, ratings, and Early Access description | Medium | Match the reviewed build, disclosures, territories, and content ratings. Confirm onboarding and review timing. | Yes for legal claims and contract; routine settings can be handled operationally. |
| Federal factual-source citation gaps | Low | Publish agency, dataset, file or series, vintage, access date, and transformation credits without implying endorsement. | Routine work does not need counsel; final release still does. |
